work experience or transportation, physical or mental health issues, no access to care for children or family member, etc.</text:p><text:p text:style-name="P326"/><text:list text:style-name="LFO33" text:continue-numbering="true"><text:list-item><text:p text:style-name="P327">None: Client does not have barrier to employment.</text:p></text:list-item><text:list-item><text:p text:style-name="P328">Lack of labour force attachment: a client who has been out of the job market for more than 3 years.</text:p></text:list-item><text:list-item><text:p text:style-name="P329">Lack of work experience: Client has little or no work experience</text:p></text:list-item><text:list-item><text:p text:style-name="P330">Lack of transportation: Client who does not have access to any type of transportation to get to their place of employment or to a counsellor.</text:p></text:list-item><text:list-item><text:p text:style-name="P331">Remoteness: Client lives in a remote area that has little or no access to job opportunities or no suitable jobs locally.</text:p></text:list-item><text:list-item><text:p text:style-name="P332">Language: Client lacks fluency in the language required for the local job market.</text:p></text:list-item><text:list-item><text:p text:style-name="P333">Education: Client who has insufficient education (i.e. less than high school).</text:p></text:list-item><text:list-item><text:p text:style-name="P334">Economic: Client does not have financial resources to purchase required equipment (boots, uniforms); costs for relocation, etc., needed to obtain employment.</text:p></text:list-item><text:list-item><text:p text:style-name="P335">Dependent care: Client does not have access to care for children or family member.</text:p></text:list-item><text:list-item><text:p text:style-name="P336">Lack of marketable skills: As a result of a shift in labour market demand, the client does not have the required marketable skills (i.e. IT innovation).</text:p></text:list-item><text:list-item><text:p text:style-name="P337">Client has a physical or mental health barrier</text:p></text:list-item><text:list-item><text:p text:style-name="P338">Other barrier not listed above: Client who identifies a barrier not provided in the list.<text:s/></text:p></text:list-item></text:list></table:table-cell><table:table-cell table:style-name="TableCell339"><text:list text:style-name="LFO30" list.<text:s/></text:p><text:p text:style-name="P542"/><text:p text:style-name="P543">4 – Skills Development – Essential Skills</text:p><text:p text:style-name="P544"/><text:p text:style-name="P545">As mentioned at the National Data Workshop, the NOC should not be required for the Essential skills intervention. Most of these interventions are not related to a specific occupation.</text:p></table:table-cell></table:table-row></table:table><text:p text:style-name="P546"/><text:p text:style-name="P547"/><text:soft-page-break/><text:p text:style-name="P548">Element to be Removed / Deactivated</text:p><text:p text:style-name="P549"/><table:table table:style-name="Table550"><table:table-columns><table:table-column table:style-name="TableColumn551"/><table:table-column table:style-name="TableColumn552"/><table:table-column table:style-name="TableColumn553"/><table:table-column table:style-name="TableColumn554"/></table:table-columns><table:table-row table:style-name="TableRow555"><table:table-cell table:style-name="TableCell556"><text:p text:style-name="P557">Element</text:p></table:table-cell><table:table-cell table:style-name="TableCell558"><text:p text:style-name="P559">Description</text:p></table:table-cell><table:table-cell table:style-name="TableCell560"><text:p text:style-name="P561">Validation Rule</text:p></table:table-cell><table:table-cell table:style-name="TableCell562"><text:p text:style-name="P563">Rational<text:s/>to remove the element</text:p></table:table-cell></table:table-row><table:table-row table:style-name="TableRow564"><table:table-cell table:style-name="TableCell565"><text:p text:style-name="P566">Telephone Number</text:p></table:table-cell><table:table-cell table:style-name="TableCell567"><text:p text:style-name="P568">Specifies the client’s telephone number. <text:s/>The telephone number entered should be the telephone number that is most likely to reach the client. <text:s/>For example: If a client has a cell phone number and home number, but is can be easily reached at his/her cell phone number then the cell phone number should be entered.</text:p><text:p text:style-name="P569"/><text:p text:style-name="P570">If the client does not have a telephone number, “No Telephone” must be used. Please do not input your organization’s telephone number.</text:p><text:p text:style-name="P571"/></table:table-cell><table:table-cell table:style-name="TableCell572"><text:p text:style-name="P573"/></table:table-cell><table:table-cell table:style-name="TableCell574"><text:p text:style-name="P575"/></table:table-cell></table:table-row><table:table-row table:style-name="TableRow576"><table:table-cell table:style-name="TableCell577"><text:p text:style-name="P578">Responsibility Centre</text:p></table:table-cell><table:table-cell table:style-name="TableCell579"><text:p text:style-name="P580">System derived (based on and not part of data exchange (i.e. not uploaded).</text:p></table:table-cell><table:table-cell table:style-name="TableCell581"><text:p text:style-name="P582"/></table:table-cell><table:table-cell table:style-name="TableCell583"><text:p text:style-name="P584"/></table:table-cell></table:table-row><table:table-row table:style-name="TableRow585"><table:table-cell table:style-name="TableCell586"><text:p text:style-name="P587">Province of Education <text:s/></text:p><text:list text:style-name="LFO16" text:continue-numbering="true"><text:list-item><text:p text:style-name="P588">Newfoundland / text:style-name="P596">Alberta</text:p></text:list-item><text:list-item><text:p text:style-name="P597">British Columbia</text:p></text:list-item><text:list-item><text:p table:style-name="TableCell602"><text:p text:style-name="P603">Province (or area outside Canada) in which highest level of education was attained.</text:p></table:table-cell><table:table-cell table:style-name="TableCell604"><text:p text:style-name="P605"/></table:table-cell><table:table-cell table:style-name="TableCell606"><text:p text:style-name="P607"/></table:table-cell></table:table-row><text:soft-page-break/><table:table-row table:style-name="TableRow608"><table:table-cell table:style-name="TableCell609"><text:p text:style-name="P610">Action Plan Childcare Need</text:p><text:list text:style-name="LFO22" text:continue-numbering="true"><text:list-item><text:p text:style-name="P611">No</text:p></text:list-item><text:list-item><text:p text:style-name="P612"><text:span text:style-name="T613">Yes</text:span></text:p></text:list-item></text:list></table:table-cell><table:table-cell table:style-name="TableCell614"><text:p text:style-name="P615">Is childcare required for this Action Plan?</text:p></table:table-cell><table:table-cell table:style-name="TableCell616"><text:list text:style-name="LFO31" text:continue-numbering="true"><text:list-item><text:p text:style-name="P617">When the “Action Plan Childcare Need” is "No" the “Action Plan Child Care Funded” must be "Not Applicable"</text:p></text:list-item><text:list-item><text:p text:style-name="P618">When the “Action Plan Childcare Need” is "Yes" the “Action Plan Child Care Funded” must not be "Not Applicable"</text:p></text:list-item></text:list></table:table-cell><table:table-cell table:style-name="TableCell619"><text:p text:style-name="P620"/></table:table-cell></table:table-row><table:table-row table:style-name="TableRow621"><table:table-cell table:style-name="TableCell622"><text:p text:style-name="P623">Action Plan Childcare Funding</text:p><text:list text:style-name="LFO23" text:continue-numbering="true"><text:list-item><text:p text:style-name="P624">Not applicable</text:p></text:list-item><text:list-item><text:p text:style-name="P625">FNICCI</text:p></text:list-item><text:list-item><text:p text:style-name="P626">EI/CRF</text:p></text:list-item><text:list-item><text:p text:style-name="P627">Provincial funding or subsidy</text:p></text:list-item><text:list-item><text:p text:style-name="P628">No funding received</text:p></text:list-item><text:list-item><text:p text:style-name="P629">Daycare space not available</text:p></text:list-item><text:list-item><text:p text:style-name="P630"><text:span text:style-name="T631">Assisted by family/self-funded</text:span></text:p></text:list-item></text:list></table:table-cell><table:table-cell table:style-name="TableCell632"><text:p text:style-name="P633">Type of financial support allocated to childcare.</text:p><text:list text:style-name="LFO35" text:continue-numbering="true"><text:list-item><text:p text:style-name="P634">Not applicable: answered no to Action Plan Childcare Need</text:p></text:list-item><text:list-item><text:p text:style-name="P635">No funding received: Client is eligible but there is no funding available.</text:p></text:list-item><text:list-item><text:p text:style-name="P636">Assisted by family/self-funded: denotes childcare that is being provided by a family member or client has sought assistance from another source not listed.</text:p></text:list-item></text:list></table:table-cell><table:table-cell table:style-name="TableCell637"><text:list text:style-name="LFO31" text:continue-numbering="true"><text:list-item><text:p text:style-name="P638">When the “Action Plan Childcare Need” is "No" the “Action Plan Child Care Funded” must be "Not Applicable"</text:p></text:list-item><text:list-item><text:p text:style-name="P639">When the “Action Plan Childcare Need” is "Yes" the “Action Plan Child Care Funded” must not be "Not Applicable"</text:p></text:list-item></text:list></table:table-cell><table:table-cell table:style-name="TableCell640"><text:p text:style-name="P641"/></table:table-cell></table:table-row><table:table-row table:style-name="TableRow642"><table:table-cell table:style-name="TableCell643"><text:p text:style-name="P644">Action Plan Cost</text:p></table:table-cell><table:table-cell table:style-name="TableCell645"><text:p text:style-name="P646">System derived (calculated from associated Intervention Costs) and not part of data exchange (i.e. not uploaded).</text:p></table:table-cell><table:table-cell table:style-name="TableCell647"><text:p text:style-name="P648"/></table:table-cell><table:table-cell table:style-name="TableCell649"><text:p text:style-name="P650"/></table:table-cell></table:table-row><text:soft-page-break/><table:table-row table:style-name="TableRow651"><table:table-cell table:style-name="TableCell652"><text:p text:style-name="P653">Intervention Duration</text:p></table:table-cell><table:table-cell table:style-name="TableCell654"><text:p text:style-name="P655">The total number of days a client is involved in an intervention. Do not count days where no action was required on the part of the client. <text:s/>For example: A client is required to go on a 4 day course to attain a driver’s license. <text:s/>The course is only available on the weekends. <text:s/>Therefore it will take the client two weeks to complete the course, but was only required to be in class for 4 days. <text:s/>The duration entered should be 4 days as opposed to 14 days (2 weeks).<text:s/></text:p><text:p text:style-name="P656"/><text:p text:style-name="P657">Note: <text:s/>The intervention duration amount can be an estimated amount; especially if the intervention duration is entered before an intervention is completed by the client (ex: the client may miss a few days due to illness or other issues). <text:s/>The duration amount does not have to be changed for every day a client does not attend or misses a course or work day, but it is preferred to have an accurate number entered. <text:s/>Thus it is recommended that the intervention duration is entered after the client has completed an intervention in order for the exact duration amount to be entered. <text:s/></text:p></table:table-cell><table:table-cell table:style-name="TableCell658"><text:list text:style-name="LFO31" text:continue-numbering="true"><text:list-item><text:p text:style-name="P659">The “Intervention Duration” must not be longer the time span between the “Intervention Start Date” and the “Intervention End Date”</text:p></text:list-item><text:list-item><text:p text:style-name="P660">When the “Action Plan Result Date” is provided the “Intervention Duration” is required</text:p></text:list-item></text:list></table:table-cell><table:table-cell table:style-name="TableCell661"><text:p text:style-name="P662"/></table:table-cell></table:table-row><text:soft-page-break/><table:table-row table:style-name="TableRow663"><table:table-cell table:style-name="TableCell664"><text:p text:style-name="P665">Intervention Cost</text:p></table:table-cell><table:table-cell table:style-name="TableCell666"><text:p text:style-name="P667">Budgeted costs are the approved budget for a particular client or project (at the time application was approved). <text:s/>For example, an intervention cost could include the price of a course and the transportation costs to get the client to the course. <text:s/>These are to be added up and included in the intervention cost. <text:s/>In essence, the amount is the sum of the budgeted costs of the intervention up to the point of data input. Regular staff, administration, and office costs are not to be inputted or added to approved budgeted costs.</text:p><text:p text:style-name="P668"/><text:p text:style-name="P669">For a client in project-based training, budgeted costs are to be calculated by dividing total approved budget for the project by the number of clients in the project at the time of intervention start date.</text:p><text:p text:style-name="P670"/><text:p text:style-name="P671">Note for clients who may be on a targeted wage subsidy intervention: <text:s/>The intervention cost can be an estimated amount; especially if the intervention cost is entered before an intervention is completed by the client (ex: the client may miss a few days due illness or other issues). <text:s/>The intervention cost does not have to be adjusted for every day a client does not attend or misses a course or work day, but it is preferred to have an accurate amount entered. <text:s/>However it is recommended that the intervention cost is entered after the client has completed an intervention in order for the exact cost to be entered. <text:s/></text:p></table:table-cell><table:table-cell table:style-name="TableCell672"><text:list text:style-name="LFO31" text:continue-numbering="true"><text:list-item><text:p text:style-name="P673">When the “Action Plan Result Date” is provided the “Intervention Cost” is required</text:p></text:list-item><text:list-item><text:p text:style-name="P674">When the “Intervention End Date” is provided the “Intervention Cost” is required</text:p></text:list-item></text:list></table:table-cell><table:table-cell table:style-name="TableCell675"><text:p text:style-name="P676"/></table:table-cell></table:table-row></table:table><text:p text:style-name="P677"/></office:text></office:body></office:document-content>
